===== M0006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 146-169
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
146 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
147 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
148 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
149 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
150 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
151 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
152 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
153 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
154 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
155 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
156 |           return false;
157 |         }
158 |       } else {
159 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
160 |           return false;
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
164 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
165 |     }
166 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
167 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
168 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
169 |   }

185 |   @Override
186 |   public int hashCode() {
187 |     int hashCode = 0;
188 |     if (returnType != null) {
189 |       hashCode += returnType.hashCode();
190 |     }
191 |     if (returnTypeInferred) {
192 |       hashCode += 1;
193 |     }
194 |     if (parameters != null) {
195 |       Node param = parameters.getFirstChild();
196 |       while (param != null) {
197 |         JSType paramType = param.getJSType();
198 |         if (paramType != null) {

200 |         }
201 |         param = param.getNext();
202 |       }
203 |     }
204 |     return hashCode;
205 |   }

/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {
458 |         final String defaultResource
459 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
460 | 
461 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
462 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 | 
467 |         String formatString;
468 |         try {
469 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
471 |             throw new RuntimeException
472 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
473 |         }
474 | 
475 |         /*
476 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
477 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */
480 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
483 |     }

/**
   * Declares a variable.
   *
   * @param name name of the variable
   * @param nameNode the NAME node declaring the variable
   * @param type the variable's type
   * @param input the input in which this variable is defined.
   * @param inferred Whether this variable's type is inferred (as opposed
   *     to declared).
   */
Method code:
386 |   Var declare(String name, Node nameNode,
387 |       JSType type, CompilerInput input, boolean inferred) {
388 |     Preconditions.checkState(name != null && name.length() > 0);
389 | 
390 |     // Make sure that it's declared only once
391 |     Preconditions.checkState(vars.get(name) == null);
392 |

394 |     var.name = name;
395 |     var.nameNode = nameNode;
396 |     var.type = type;
397 |     var.scope = this;

406 |     var.info = info;

===== M0037 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 987-1100
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a typed variable. The defining node will be annotated with the
     * variable's type of {@link JSTypeNative#UNKNOWN_TYPE} if its type is
     * inferred.
     *
     * Slots may be any variable or any qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     */
Method code:
987 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, JSType type, boolean inferred) {
988 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(inferred || type != null);
989 | 
990 |       // Only allow declarations of NAMEs and qualfied names.
991 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = false;
992 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
993 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
994 |             parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
995 |             parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
996 |             parent.getType() == Token.LP ||
997 |             parent.getType() == Token.CATCH);
998 |         shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = scope.isGlobal() &&
999 |             (parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
1000 |              parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
1001 |       } else {
1002 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
1003 |             n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
1004 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN ||
1005 |              parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT));
1006 |       }
1007 |       String variableName = n.getQualifiedName();
1008 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1009 | 
1010 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1011 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1012 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1013 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1014 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1015 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1016 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1017 | 
1018 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1019 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1020 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1021 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1022 |         }
1023 |       }
1024 | 
1025 |       // declared in closest scope?
1026 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1027 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1028 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1029 |             sourceName, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1030 |       } else {
1031 |         if (!inferred) {
1032 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1033 |         }
1034 |         CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
1035 |         boolean isExtern = input.isExtern();
1036 |         Var newVar =
1037 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1038 | 
1039 |         if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1040 |           ObjectType globalThis =
1041 |               typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
1042 |           if (inferred) {
1043 |             globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1044 |                 type == null ?
1045 |                     getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1046 |                     type,
1047 |                 isExtern, n);
1048 |           } else {
1049 |             globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, isExtern, n);
1050 |           }
1051 |         }
1052 | 
1053 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1054 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1055 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1056 |               (initialValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT ||
1057 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1058 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1059 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1060 |           }
1061 |         }
1062 | 
1063 |         // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1064 |         if (type instanceof FunctionType &&
1065 |             // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1066 |             !type.isEmptyType()) {
1067 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
1068 |           if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1069 |               !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1070 |             // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1071 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1072 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName + ".prototype", n,
1073 |                 fnType.getPrototype(), input,
1074 |                 /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1075 |                 superClassCtor == null ||
1076 |                 superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1077 |                     getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1078 | 
1079 |             // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1080 |             // it constructs itself.
1081 |             if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1082 |                 !isExtern &&
1083 |                 // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1084 |                 // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1085 |                 // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1086 |                 // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1087 |                 // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1088 |                 // this.
1089 |                 variableName.equals(
1090 |                     fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1091 |               compiler.report(
1092 |                   JSError.make(sourceName, n,

===== M0048 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 779-793
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
779 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
780 |     switch (n.getType()) {
781 |       case Token.DO:
782 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
783 |       case Token.FOR:
784 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
785 |           return n;
786 |         }
787 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
788 |       case Token.LABEL:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
790 |       default:
791 |         return n;
792 |     }
793 |   }

   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.

893 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
894 |     switch (n.getType()) {
895 |       case Token.CALL:
896 |       case Token.GETPROP:
897 |       case Token.GETELEM:
898 |       case Token.THROW:
899 |       case Token.NEW:

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 128-149
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
128 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
129 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
130 |     String methodName = null;
131 |     if (callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
132 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
133 |     } else if (callName.getType() == Token.NAME) {
134 |       String name = callName.getString();
135 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
136 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
137 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
138 |       }
139 |     }
140 | 
141 |     if (methodName != null) {
142 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
143 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
144 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
145 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
146 |       }
147 |     }
148 |     return null;
149 |   }

===== M0057 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 192-304
Documentation:

Method code:
192 |   @Override
193 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
194 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
195 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
196 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
197 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
198 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
199 |     // worthwhile.
200 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
201 |     Node condition = null;
202 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
203 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
204 | 
205 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
206 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
208 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
209 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
210 | 
211 |       switch (branch) {
212 |         case ON_TRUE:
213 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
214 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
215 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
216 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
217 | 
218 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
219 | 
220 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
221 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
222 |             }
223 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
224 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
225 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
226 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
227 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
228 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
229 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
230 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
231 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
232 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
233 |                 }
234 |               }
235 |               redeclare(informed, item.getString(), iterKeyType);
236 |             }
237 |             newScope = informed;
238 |             break;
239 |           }
240 | 
241 |           // FALL THROUGH
242 | 
243 |         case ON_FALSE:
244 |           if (condition == null) {
245 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
246 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
247 |               condition = source;
248 | 
249 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
250 |               // of the loop.
251 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
252 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
253 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
254 |               }
255 |             }
256 |           }
257 | 
258 |           if (condition != null) {
259 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
260 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
261 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
262 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
263 |               // scope on false.
264 |               //
265 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
266 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
267 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
268 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
269 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
270 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
271 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
272 | 
273 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
274 |               // of the loop.
275 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
276 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
277 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
278 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
279 |               }
280 |               newScope =
281 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
282 |                       condition,
283 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
284 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
285 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
286 |             } else {
287 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
288 |               // of the loop.
289 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
290 |                 conditionFlowScope =
291 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
292 |               }
293 |               newScope =
294 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
295 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
296 |             }
297 |           }
298 |           break;
299 |       }
300 | 
301 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
302 |     }
303 |     return result;
304 |   }

===== M0058 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1062-1096
Documentation:

Method code:
1062 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1063 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1064 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1065 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1066 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1067 |     if (var != null) {
1068 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1069 |       if (varType != null) {
1070 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1071 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
1072 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
1073 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1074 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1075 |         } else {
1076 |           return varType;
1077 |         }
1078 |       }
1079 |     }
1080 | 
1081 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1082 |     if (objType != null) {
1083 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1084 |     }
1085 | 
1086 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType()) &&
1087 |         qualifiedName != null) {
1088 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1089 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1090 |       if (regType != null) {
1091 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1092 |       }
1093 |     }
1094 | 
1095 |     return propertyType;
1096 |   }

===== M0075 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 773-871
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the jsdoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
773 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
774 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
775 |         @Nullable String name,
776 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
777 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
778 | 
779 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
780 | 
781 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
782 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
783 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
784 |         if (var != null && var.getType() instanceof FunctionType) {
785 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = (FunctionType) var.getType();
786 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
787 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
788 |             functionType = aliasedType;
789 | 
790 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
791 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
792 |             }
793 |           }
794 |         }
795 |       }
796 | 
797 |       if (functionType == null) {
798 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
799 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
800 |             rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION;
801 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
802 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
803 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
804 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
805 | 
806 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
807 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
808 | 
809 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
810 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
811 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
812 |             functionType = (FunctionType) type;
813 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
814 |           }
815 |         }
816 | 
817 |         if (functionType == null) {
818 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
819 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
820 |           if (lvalueNode != null &&
821 |               lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
822 |               lvalueNode.isQualifiedName()) {
823 |             Var var = scope.getVar(
824 |                 lvalueNode.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName());
825 |             if (var != null) {
826 |               ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(var.getType());
827 |               if (ownerType != null) {
828 |                 String propName = lvalueNode.getLastChild().getString();
829 |                 overriddenPropType = findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
830 |               }
831 |             }
832 |           }
833 | 
834 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
835 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
836 |                   scope)
837 |               .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
838 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
839 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
840 |               .inferReturnType(info)
841 |               .inferInheritance(info);
842 | 
843 |           // Infer the context type.
844 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
845 |           if (lvalueNode != null &&
846 |               lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
847 |             Node objNode = lvalueNode.getFirstChild();
848 |             if (objNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
849 |                 objNode.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
850 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getFirstChild());
851 |               searchedForThisType = true;
852 |             } else if (objNode.getType() == Token.THIS) {
853 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getJSType());
854 |               searchedForThisType = true;
855 |             }
856 |           }
857 | 
858 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
859 |             builder.inferThisType(info, (Node) null);
860 |           }
861 | 
862 |           functionType = builder
863 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
864 |               .inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(fnBlock)
865 |               .buildAndRegister();
866 |         }
867 |       }
868 | 
869 |       // all done
870 |       return functionType;
871 |     }

/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1658 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1659 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1660 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1661 |     }
1662 | 
1663 |     Node typeName = newStringNode(stream.getString());
1664 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1665 |       next();
1666 |       skipEOLs();
1667 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1668 |       if (memberType != null) {
1669 |         typeName.addChildToFront(memberType);
1670 | 
1671 |         skipEOLs();
1672 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1673 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1674 |         }
1675 | 
1676 |         next();
1677 |       }
1678 |     }
1679 |     return typeName;
1680 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1456-1486
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1456 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1457 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1458 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1459 | 
1460 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1461 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1462 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1463 |       return;
1464 |     }
1465 | 
1466 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1467 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1468 |     if (childType instanceof FunctionType) {
1469 |       FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) childType;
1470 | 
1471 |       // Non-native constructors should never be called directly.
1472 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1473 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType()) {
1474 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1475 |       }
1476 | 
1477 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1478 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1479 |     } else {
1480 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1481 |     }
1482 | 
1483 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1484 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1485 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1486 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 466-535
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
466 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
467 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
468 |     if (argsParent == null) {
469 |       if (info == null) {
470 |         return this;
471 |       } else {
472 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
473 |       }
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     // arguments
477 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
478 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
479 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
483 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
484 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
485 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
486 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
487 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
488 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
489 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
490 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
491 | 
492 |       // type from JSDocInfo
493 |       JSType parameterType = null;
494 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
495 |       boolean isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
496 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
497 |         parameterType =
498 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
499 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
500 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
501 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
502 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
503 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
504 |       } else {
505 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
506 |       }
507 | 
508 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
509 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
510 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
511 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
512 |         }
513 |         foundTemplateType = true;
514 |       }
515 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
516 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
517 |           isOptionalParam,
518 |           isVarArgs);
519 | 
520 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
521 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
522 |       }
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
526 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
527 |     }
528 | 
529 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
530 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
534 |     return this;
535 |   }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 82-178
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
83 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
84 | 
85 |     switch (n.getType()) {
86 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
87 |       case Token.NAME:
88 |         if (parent == null) {
89 |           return;
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
93 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
94 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
95 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
96 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
97 |           return;
98 |         }
99 | 
100 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
101 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
102 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
103 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
104 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
105 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
106 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
107 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
108 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
109 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
110 |         if (docInfo == null &&
111 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
112 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
113 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
114 |         }
115 | 
116 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
117 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
118 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
119 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
120 |         }
121 | 
122 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
123 |         // we can do.
124 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
125 |           return;
126 |         }
127 | 
128 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
129 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
130 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
131 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 | 
135 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
136 |         break;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.GETPROP:
139 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
140 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
141 |         //
142 |         // 1)
143 |         // /** @deprecated */
144 |         // obj.prop = ...
145 |         //
146 |         // 2)
147 |         // /** @deprecated */
148 |         // obj.prop;
149 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
150 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
151 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
152 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
153 |           if (docInfo == null) {
154 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
155 |           }
156 |           if (docInfo != null) {
157 |             ObjectType lhsType =
158 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
159 |             if (lhsType != null) {
160 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
161 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
162 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
163 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo, inExterns);
164 |               }
165 | 
166 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
167 |               ObjectType propType =
168 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
169 |               if (propType != null) {
170 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
171 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
172 |               }
173 |             }
174 |           }
175 |         }
176 |         break;
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0103 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 632-658
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
632 |   @Override
633 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
634 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
635 |       return false;
636 |     }
637 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
638 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
639 |       return false;
640 |     }
641 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
642 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
643 |         return this == that;
644 |       }
645 |       return false;
646 |     }
647 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
648 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
649 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
650 |       }
651 |       return false;
652 |     }
653 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
654 |       return false;
655 |     }
656 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
657 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
658 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.findOverriddenFunction(ObjectType,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 876-896
Documentation:
/**
     * Find the function that's being overridden on this type, if any.
     */
Method code:
876 |     private FunctionType findOverriddenFunction(
877 |         ObjectType ownerType, String propName) {
878 |       // First, check to see if the property is implemented
879 |       // on a superclass.
880 |       JSType propType = ownerType.getPropertyType(propName);
881 |       if (propType instanceof FunctionType) {
882 |         return (FunctionType) propType;
883 |       } else {
884 |         // If it's not, then check to see if it's implemented
885 |         // on an implemented interface.
886 |         for (ObjectType iface :
887 |                  ownerType.getCtorImplementedInterfaces()) {
888 |           propType = iface.getPropertyType(propName);
889 |           if (propType instanceof FunctionType) {
890 |             return (FunctionType) propType;
891 |           }
892 |         }
893 |       }
894 | 
895 |       return null;
896 |     }

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1047 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
1048 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1049 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1050 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
1051 | 
1052 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1053 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1054 | 
1055 |     jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endCharno,
1056 |         true);
1057 |     return typeNode;
1058 |   }

===== M0107 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1266-1370
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1266 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1267 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1268 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1269 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1270 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1271 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1272 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1273 | 
1274 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1275 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1276 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1277 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1278 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1279 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1280 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1281 |         if (qVar != null) {
1282 |           if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1283 |             // Just ignore assigns to declared prototypes.
1284 |             return;
1285 |           }
1286 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1287 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1288 |           }
1289 |         }
1290 |       }
1291 | 
1292 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1293 |       // 1) @type annnotation / @enum annotation
1294 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1295 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1296 |       // 4) ASSIGN to anything else
1297 |       //
1298 |       // 1 and 3 are declarations, 4 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1299 |       // the function has not been declared before.
1300 |       //
1301 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1302 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1303 | 
1304 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3
1305 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredPropType(t, info, n, rhsValue);
1306 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1307 |         // Determining type for #4
1308 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1309 |       }
1310 | 
1311 |       if (valueType == null) {
1312 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
1313 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1314 |               n,
1315 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1316 |               ownerName));
1317 |         }
1318 | 
1319 |         return;
1320 |       }
1321 | 
1322 |       boolean inferred = true;
1323 |       if (info != null) {
1324 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3
1325 |         inferred = !(info.hasType() || info.hasEnumParameterType() ||
1326 |             FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1327 |       }
1328 | 
1329 |       if (inferred) {
1330 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1331 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1332 |             rhsValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1333 |             !scope.isDeclared(qName, false));
1334 |       }
1335 | 
1336 |       if (!inferred) {
1337 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1338 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1339 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1340 |           // declared yet.
1341 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1342 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1343 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1344 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1345 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1346 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1347 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, isExtern, n);
1348 |           }
1349 |         }
1350 | 
1351 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1352 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1353 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1354 |       } else if (rhsValue != null &&
1355 |           rhsValue.getType() == Token.TRUE) {
1356 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1357 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1358 |         if (ownerType instanceof FunctionType) {
1359 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ((FunctionType) ownerType).getTypeOfThis();
1360 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1361 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1362 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1363 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1364 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1365 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE),
1366 |                 true);
1367 |           }
1368 |         }
1369 |       }
1370 |     }

===== M0117 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 420-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */
Method code:
956 |   public final JSType resolve(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
957 |     if (resolved) {
958 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Check to see if resolve() looped back on itself.
959 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);
960 |       if (resolveResult == null) {
961 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
962 |       }
963 |       return resolveResult;
964 |     }
965 |     resolved = true;
966 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
967 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
968 |     return resolveResult;
969 |   }

===== M0155 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 150-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
150 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
151 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
152 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
153 |       case Token.BLOCK:
154 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
155 |       case Token.TRY:
156 |       case Token.FINALLY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0156 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1155-1166
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1155 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1156 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1157 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1158 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1159 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1160 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1161 |       } else {
1162 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1163 |       }
1164 |     }
1165 |     return builder.build();
1166 |   }

===== M0158 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 422-466
Documentation:

Method code:
422 |   @Override
423 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
424 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
425 |       return true;
426 |     }
427 | 
428 |     // Union types
429 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
430 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
431 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
432 |       return false;
433 |     }
434 | 
435 |     // record types
436 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
437 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Interfaces
441 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
442 |     // to the interface instance.
443 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
444 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
445 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
446 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
447 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
448 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // other prototype based objects
455 |     if (that != null) {
456 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
457 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
458 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
459 |         // to avoid guessing.
460 |         return true;
461 |       }
462 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
463 |     }
464 | 
465 |     return false;
466 |   }

/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

===== M0169 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 587-617
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
587 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
588 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
589 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
590 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
591 |     if (isOptional) {
592 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
593 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
594 |       // error can be reported.
595 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
596 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
597 |         emittedWarning = true;
598 |       }
599 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
600 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
601 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
602 |         emittedWarning = true;
603 |       }
604 |     } else {
605 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
606 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
607 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
608 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
609 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
610 |         } else {
611 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
612 |         }
613 |         emittedWarning = true;
614 |       }
615 |     }
616 |     return emittedWarning;
617 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)

Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
602 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
603 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
604 |     if (typeSet == null) {
605 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
606 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
607 |     }
608 | 
609 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
610 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
611 | 
612 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
613 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
614 |   }

===== M0204 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 311-341
Documentation:
/**
   * If we haven't found a return value yet, try to look at the "return"
   * statements in the function.
   */
Method code:
311 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(
312 |       @Nullable Node functionBlock) {
313 |     if (functionBlock == null || compiler.getInput(sourceName).isExtern()) {
314 |       return this;
315 |     }
316 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(functionBlock.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
317 |     if (returnType == null) {
318 |       boolean hasNonEmptyReturns = false;
319 |       List<Node> worklist = Lists.newArrayList(functionBlock);
320 |       while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
321 |         Node current = worklist.remove(worklist.size() - 1);
322 |         int cType = current.getType();
323 |         if (cType == Token.RETURN && current.getFirstChild() != null) {
324 |           hasNonEmptyReturns = true;
325 |           break;
326 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(current) ||
327 |             NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
328 |           for (Node child = current.getFirstChild();
329 |                child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
330 |             worklist.add(child);
331 |           }
332 |         }
333 |       }
334 | 
335 |       if (!hasNonEmptyReturns) {
336 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
337 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
338 |       }
339 |     }
340 |     return this;
341 |   }

===== M0222 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 681-717
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
681 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
682 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
683 |         fnName, sourceNode, parametersNode, returnType);
684 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
685 | 
686 |     if (existingType != null) {
687 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType instanceof InstanceObjectType;
688 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
689 |         FunctionType existingFn =
690 |             isInstanceObject ?
691 |             ((InstanceObjectType) existingType).getConstructor() :
692 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
693 | 
694 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
695 |           existingFn.setSource(sourceNode);
696 |         }
697 | 
698 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
699 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
700 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
701 |         }
702 | 
703 |         return existingFn;
704 |       } else {
705 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
706 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
707 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
708 |       }
709 |     }
710 | 
711 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
712 | 
713 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
714 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
715 |     }
716 |     return fnType;
717 |   }

/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1726 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1727 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1728 |       return getString();
1729 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1730 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1731 |       if (left == null) {
1732 |         return null;
1733 |       }
1734 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1735 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1736 |       return "this";
1737 |     } else {
1738 |       return null;
1739 |     }
1740 |   }

===== M0235 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
233 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
234 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
235 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
236 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
237 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
238 | 
239 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
240 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
241 |       // if (this.x == null) {
242 |       //   this.initializeX();
243 |       //   this.x.foo();
244 |       // }
245 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
246 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
247 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
248 |       //
249 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
250 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
251 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
252 |         return true;
253 |       }
254 | 
255 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
256 |       return false;
257 |     }
258 |     return true;
259 |   }

55 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
56 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
57 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
58 |       addGuards(Lists.reverse(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards));
59 |     } else {

64 |       this.guards.add(index, guard);
