===== M0252 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 149-885
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
149 |   boolean parse() {
150 |     int lineno;
151 |     int charno;
152 | 
153 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
154 |     JSTypeExpression type;
155 | 
156 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
157 |     skipEOLs();
158 | 
159 |     JsDocToken token = next();
160 | 
161 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
162 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
163 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
164 |       token = blockInfo.token;
165 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
166 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
167 |       }
168 |     } else {
169 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
170 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
171 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
172 |         // what it was.
173 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
174 |       }
175 |     }
176 | 
177 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
178 |     retry: for (;;) {
179 |       switch (token) {
180 |         case ANNOTATION:
181 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
182 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
183 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
184 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
185 | 
186 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
187 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
188 |             if (annotation == null) {
189 |               parser.addWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
190 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
191 |             } else {
192 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
193 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
194 | 
195 |               switch (annotation) {
196 |                 case AUTHOR:
197 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
198 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
199 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
200 | 
201 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
202 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
203 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
204 |                     } else {
205 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
206 |                     }
207 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
208 |                   } else {
209 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
210 |                   }
211 |                   continue retry;
212 | 
213 |                 case CONSTANT:
214 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
215 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
216 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
217 |                   }
218 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
219 |                   continue retry;
220 | 
221 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
222 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
223 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
224 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
225 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |                     } else {
227 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
228 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
229 |                     }
230 |                   }
231 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
232 |                   continue retry;
233 | 
234 |                 case DEPRECATED:
235 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
236 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
237 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
238 |                   }
239 | 
240 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
241 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
242 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
243 | 
244 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
245 | 
246 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
247 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
248 |                   }
249 | 
250 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
251 |                   continue retry;
252 | 
253 |                 case INTERFACE:
254 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
255 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
256 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
257 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
258 |                     } else {
259 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
260 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
261 |                     }
262 |                   }
263 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
264 |                   continue retry;
265 | 
266 |                 case DESC:
267 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
268 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
269 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
270 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
271 |                     continue retry;
272 |                   } else {
273 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
274 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
275 | 
276 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
277 | 
278 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
279 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
280 |                     continue retry;
281 |                   }
282 | 
283 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
284 |                   String fileOverview = "";
285 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
286 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
287 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
288 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
289 | 
290 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
291 | 
292 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
293 |                   } else {
294 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
295 |                   }
296 | 
297 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
298 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
299 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
300 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
301 |                   }
302 |                   continue retry;
303 | 
304 |                 case LICENSE:
305 |                 case PRESERVE:
306 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
307 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
308 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
309 | 
310 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
311 | 
312 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
313 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
314 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
315 |                     }
316 |                   }
317 | 
318 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
319 |                   continue retry;
320 | 
321 |                 case ENUM:
322 |                   token = next();
323 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
324 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
325 | 
326 |                   type = null;
327 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
328 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
329 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
330 |                   }
331 | 
332 |                   if (type == null) {
333 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
334 |                   }
335 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
336 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
337 |                   }
338 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
339 |                   continue retry;
340 | 
341 |                 case EXPORT:
342 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
343 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
344 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
345 |                   }
346 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
347 |                   continue retry;
348 | 
349 |                 case EXTERNS:
350 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
351 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
352 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
353 |                   }
354 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
355 |                   continue retry;
356 | 
357 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
358 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
359 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
360 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
361 |                   }
362 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
363 |                   continue retry;
364 | 
365 |                 case EXTENDS:
366 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
367 |                   skipEOLs();
368 |                   token = next();
369 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
370 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
371 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
372 | 
373 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
374 |                     token = next();
375 |                     matchingRc = true;
376 |                   }
377 | 
378 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
379 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
380 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
381 | 
382 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
383 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
384 | 
385 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
386 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
387 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
388 |                     }
389 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
390 | 
391 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
392 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordBaseType(type)) {
393 |                         parser.addWarning(
394 |                             "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
395 |                       }
396 |                     } else {
397 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
398 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
399 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
400 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
401 |                             lineno, charno);
402 |                       }
403 |                     }
404 |                     token = next();
405 |                     if (matchingRc) {
406 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
407 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
408 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
409 |                       }
410 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
411 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
412 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
413 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
414 |                     }
415 |                   } else {
416 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
417 |                   }
418 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
419 |                   continue retry;
420 | 
421 |                 case HIDDEN:
422 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
423 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
424 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
425 |                   }
426 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
427 |                   continue retry;
428 | 
429 |                 case LENDS:
430 |                   skipEOLs();
431 | 
432 |                   matchingRc = false;
433 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.LC)) {
434 |                     token = next();
435 |                     matchingRc = true;
436 |                   }
437 | 
438 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
439 |                     token = next();
440 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordLends(stream.getString())) {
441 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.incompatible",
442 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
443 |                     }
444 |                   } else {
445 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.missing",
446 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
447 |                   }
448 | 
449 |                   if (matchingRc && !match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
450 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
451 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
452 |                   }
453 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
454 |                   continue retry;
455 | 
456 |                 case MEANING:
457 |                   ExtractionInfo meaningInfo =
458 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
459 |                   String meaning = meaningInfo.string;
460 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;
461 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordMeaning(meaning)) {
462 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.meaning.extra",
463 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
464 |                   }
465 |                   continue retry;
466 | 
467 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
468 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
469 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
470 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
471 |                   }
472 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
473 |                   continue retry;
474 | 
475 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
476 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
477 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
478 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
479 |                   }
480 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
481 |                   continue retry;
482 | 
483 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
484 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
485 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
486 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
487 |                   }
488 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
489 |                   continue retry;
490 | 
491 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
492 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
493 |                   continue retry;
494 | 
495 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
496 |                 case OVERRIDE:
497 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
498 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
499 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
500 |                   }
501 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
502 |                   continue retry;
503 | 
504 |                 case THROWS:
505 |                   skipEOLs();
506 |                   token = next();
507 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
508 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
509 |                   type = null;
510 | 
511 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
512 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
513 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
514 | 
515 |                     if (type == null) {
516 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
517 |                       // recovering parsing
518 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
519 |                       continue retry;
520 |                     }
521 |                   }
522 | 
523 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
524 |                   token = current();
525 | 
526 |                   // Save the throw type.
527 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
528 | 
529 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
530 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
531 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
532 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
533 | 
534 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
535 | 
536 |                     if (description.length() > 0) {
537 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
538 |                     }
539 | 
540 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
541 |                   } else {
542 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
543 |                   }
544 |                   continue retry;
545 | 
546 |                 case PARAM:
547 |                   skipEOLs();
548 |                   token = next();
549 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
550 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
551 |                   type = null;
552 | 
553 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
554 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
555 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
556 | 
557 |                     if (type == null) {
558 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
559 |                       // recovering parsing
560 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
561 |                       continue retry;
562 |                     }
563 |                     skipEOLs();
564 |                     token = next();
565 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
566 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
567 |                   }
568 | 
569 |                   String name = null;
570 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
571 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
572 |                     token = next();
573 |                   }
574 | 
575 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
576 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
577 |                         lineno, charno);
578 |                   } else {
579 |                     name = stream.getString();
580 | 
581 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
582 |                       token = next();
583 | 
584 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter annotation.
585 |                       // It makes no sense under our type system.
586 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
587 |                         token = next();
588 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
589 |                           token = next();
590 |                         }
591 |                       }
592 | 
593 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
594 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
595 |                       } else if (type != null) {
596 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
597 |                         // already.
598 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
599 |                       }
600 |                     }
601 | 
602 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
603 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
604 |                     // for handling properties of params.
605 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
606 |                       name = null;
607 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
608 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
609 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
610 |                             lineno, charno);
611 |                       } else {
612 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
613 |                             lineno, charno);
614 |                       }
615 |                     }
616 |                   }
617 | 
618 |                   if (name == null) {
619 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
620 |                     continue retry;
621 |                   }
622 | 
623 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, lineno, charno);
624 | 
625 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
626 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
627 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
628 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
629 | 
630 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
631 | 
632 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
633 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
634 |                           paramDescription);
635 |                     }
636 | 
637 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
638 |                   } else {
639 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
640 |                   }
641 |                   continue retry;
642 | 
643 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
644 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
645 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
646 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
647 |                   }
648 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
649 |                   continue retry;
650 | 
651 |                 case PRIVATE:
652 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
653 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
654 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
655 |                   }
656 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
657 |                   continue retry;
658 | 
659 |                 case PROTECTED:
660 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
661 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
662 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
663 |                   }
664 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
665 |                   continue retry;
666 | 
667 |                 case PUBLIC:
668 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
669 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
670 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
671 |                   }
672 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
673 |                   continue retry;
674 | 
675 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
676 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
677 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
678 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
679 |                   }
680 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
681 |                   continue retry;
682 | 
683 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
684 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
685 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
686 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
687 |                   }
688 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
689 |                   continue retry;
690 | 
691 |                 case MODIFIES:
692 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
693 |                   continue retry;
694 | 
695 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
696 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
697 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
698 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
699 |                   }
700 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
701 |                   continue retry;
702 | 
703 |                 case SEE:
704 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
705 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
706 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
707 | 
708 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
709 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
710 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
711 |                     } else {
712 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
713 |                     }
714 | 
715 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
716 |                   } else {
717 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
718 |                   }
719 |                   continue retry;
720 | 
721 |                 case SUPPRESS:
722 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
723 |                   continue retry;
724 | 
725 |                 case TEMPLATE:
726 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
727 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
728 | 
729 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
730 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
731 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
732 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
733 |                       templateTypeName)) {
734 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
735 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
736 |                   }
737 | 
738 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
739 |                   continue retry;
740 | 
741 |                 case VERSION:
742 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
743 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
744 | 
745 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
746 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
747 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
748 |                   } else {
749 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
750 |                        parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
751 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
752 |                     }
753 |                   }
754 | 
755 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
756 |                   continue retry;
757 | 
758 |                 case DEFINE:
759 |                 case RETURN:
760 |                 case THIS:
761 |                 case TYPE:
762 |                 case TYPEDEF:
763 |                   skipEOLs();
764 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
765 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
766 | 
767 |                   token = next();
768 | 
769 |                   Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token, lineno, charno);
770 | 
771 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
772 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
773 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
774 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
775 |                     }
776 |                   }
777 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
778 | 
779 |                   if (type == null) {
780 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
781 |                     // recovering parsing
782 |                   } else {
783 |                     switch (annotation) {
784 |                       case DEFINE:
785 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
786 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
787 |                               lineno, charno);
788 |                         }
789 |                         break;
790 | 
791 |                       case RETURN:
792 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
793 |                           parser.addWarning(
794 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
795 |                           break;
796 |                         }
797 | 
798 |                         // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
799 |                         token = current();
800 | 
801 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
802 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
803 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
804 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
805 | 
806 |                           String returnDescription =
807 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
808 | 
809 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
810 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
811 |                                 returnDescription);
812 |                           }
813 | 
814 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
815 |                         } else {
816 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
817 |                         }
818 |                         continue retry;
819 | 
820 |                       case THIS:
821 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
822 |                           parser.addWarning(
823 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
824 |                         }
825 |                         break;
826 | 
827 |                       case TYPE:
828 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
829 |                           parser.addWarning(
830 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
831 |                         }
832 |                         break;
833 | 
834 |                       case TYPEDEF:
835 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
836 |                           parser.addWarning(
837 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
838 |                         }
839 |                         break;
840 |                     }
841 | 
842 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
843 |                   }
844 |                   continue retry;
845 |               }
846 |             }
847 |           }
848 |           break;
849 | 
850 |         case EOC:
851 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
852 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
853 |           }
854 | 
855 |           return true;
856 | 
857 |         case EOF:
858 |           // discard any accumulated information
859 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
860 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
861 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
862 |           return false;
863 | 
864 |         case EOL:
865 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
866 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
867 |           }
868 |           token = next();
869 |           continue retry;
870 | 
871 |         default:
872 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
873 |             token = next();
874 |             continue retry;
875 |           } else {
876 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
877 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
878 |             continue retry;
879 |           }
880 |       }
881 | 
882 |       // next token
883 |       token = next();
884 |     }
885 |   }

===== M0260 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 512-562
Documentation:

Method code:
512 |    @Override
513 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
514 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
515 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
516 |       if (name == null) {
517 |         name = new Name();
518 |         name.setIdentifier("");
519 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
520 |       }
521 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
522 |       Node newName = transform(name);
523 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
524 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
525 |         // declaration.
526 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
527 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
528 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
529 |         // same line as the function keyword.
530 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
531 |             functionNode.getLp();
532 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
533 |       }
534 | 
535 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
536 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
537 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
538 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
539 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
540 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
541 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
542 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
543 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
544 |       if (fnName != null) {
545 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
546 |       } else {
547 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
548 |       }
549 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
550 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
551 | 
552 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
553 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
554 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
555 |       }
556 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
557 | 
558 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
559 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
560 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
561 |      return node;
562 |     }

===== M0263 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.supAndInfHelper(JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 475-551
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the supremum or infimum of functions with other types.
   * Because sup() and inf() share a lot of logic for functions, we use
   * a single helper.
   * @param leastSuper If true, compute the supremum of {@code this} with
   *     {@code that}. Otherwise compute the infimum.
   * @return The least supertype or greatest subtype.
   */
Method code:
475 |   private JSType supAndInfHelper(JSType that, boolean leastSuper) {
476 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): When we remove the unknown type, the function types
477 |     // form a lattice with the universal constructor at the top of the lattice,
478 |     // and the LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE type at the bottom of the lattice.
479 |     //
480 |     // When we introduce the unknown type, it's much more difficult to make
481 |     // heads or tails of the partial ordering of types, because there's no
482 |     // clear hierarchy between the different components (parameter types and
483 |     // return types) in the ArrowType.
484 |     //
485 |     // Rather than make the situation more complicated by introducing new
486 |     // types (like unions of functions), we just fallback on the simpler
487 |     // approach of getting things right at the top and the bottom of the
488 |     // lattice.
489 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
490 |       if (isEquivalentTo(that)) {
491 |         return this;
492 |       }
493 | 
494 |       FunctionType other = null;
495 |       if (that instanceof FunctionType) {
496 |         other = (FunctionType) that;
497 |       }
498 | 
499 |       // If these are ordinary functions, then merge them.
500 |       // Don't do this if any of the params/return
501 |       // values are unknown, because then there will be cycles in
502 |       // their local lattice and they will merge in weird ways.
503 |       if (other != null &&
504 |           isOrdinaryFunction() && that.isOrdinaryFunction() &&
505 |           !this.call.hasUnknownParamsOrReturn() &&
506 |           !other.call.hasUnknownParamsOrReturn()) {
507 | 
508 |         // Check for the degenerate case, but double check
509 |         // that there's not a cycle.
510 |         boolean isSubtypeOfThat = this.isSubtype(that);
511 |         boolean isSubtypeOfThis = that.isSubtype(this);
512 |         if (isSubtypeOfThat && !isSubtypeOfThis) {
513 |           return leastSuper ? that : this;
514 |         } else if (isSubtypeOfThis && !isSubtypeOfThat) {
515 |           return leastSuper ? this : that;
516 |         }
517 | 
518 |         // Merge the two functions component-wise.
519 |         FunctionType merged = tryMergeFunctionPiecewise(other, leastSuper);
520 |         if (merged != null) {
521 |           return merged;
522 |         }
523 |       }
524 | 
525 |       // The function instance type is a special case
526 |       // that lives above the rest of the lattice.
527 |       JSType functionInstance = registry.getNativeType(
528 |           JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE);
529 |       if (functionInstance.isEquivalentTo(that)) {
530 |         return leastSuper ? that : this;
531 |       } else if (functionInstance.isEquivalentTo(this)) {
532 |         return leastSuper ? this : that;
533 |       }
534 | 
535 |       // In theory, we should be using the GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE as the
536 |       // greatest function. In practice, we don't because it's way too
537 |       // broad. The greatest function takes var_args None parameters, which
538 |       // means that all parameters register a type warning.
539 |       //
540 |       // Instead, we use the U2U ctor type, which has unknown type args.
541 |       FunctionType greatestFn =
542 |           registry.getNativeFunctionType(JSTypeNative.U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
543 |       FunctionType leastFn =
544 |           registry.getNativeFunctionType(JSTypeNative.LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
545 |       return leastSuper ? greatestFn : leastFn;
546 |     }
547 | 
548 |     return leastSuper ?
549 |         super.getLeastSupertype(that) :
550 |         super.getGreatestSubtype(that);
551 |   }

===== M0267 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1811-1813
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1811 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1812 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1813 |   }

===== M0271 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 371-386
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
371 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
372 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
373 |       Set<String> directives = null;
374 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
375 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
376 |         if (directives == null) {
377 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
378 |         } else {
379 |           directives.add(directive);
380 |         }
381 |       }
382 | 
383 |       if (directives != null) {

===== M0273 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)

Original range: 296-304
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
296 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
297 |       Node ret = transform(n);
298 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
299 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
300 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
301 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
302 |       }
303 |       return ret;
304 |     }

===== M0277 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);

===== M0286 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 734-776
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
734 |   @Override
735 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
736 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
737 |       return true;
738 |     }
739 | 
740 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
741 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
742 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
743 |         return true;
744 |       }
745 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
747 |         return false;
748 |       }
749 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
750 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
751 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
752 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
753 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
754 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
755 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
756 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
757 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
758 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
759 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
760 | 
761 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
762 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
763 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
764 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
765 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
766 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
767 | 
768 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
769 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
770 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
771 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
772 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
773 |     }
774 | 
775 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
776 |   }

===== M0293 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 237-288
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
237 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
238 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
239 |     if (oldType == null) {
240 |       return this;
241 |     }
242 | 
243 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
244 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
245 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
246 |       // Not a function literal.
247 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
248 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
249 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
250 |       }
251 |     } else {
252 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
253 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
254 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
255 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
256 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
257 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
258 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
259 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
260 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
261 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
262 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
263 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
264 | 
265 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
266 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
267 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
268 | 
269 |           // The subclass method might right its var_args as individual
270 |           // arguments.
271 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
272 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
273 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
274 |           }
275 |         } else {
276 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
277 |               paramBuilder,
278 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
279 |               warnedAboutArgList,
280 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
281 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
282 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
283 |         }
284 |       }
285 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
286 |     }
287 |     return this;
288 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitVar(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1341-1374
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1341 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1342 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1343 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1344 |     // merge to fix this.
1345 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1346 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1347 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1348 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1349 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1350 | 
1351 |       if (value != null) {
1352 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1353 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1354 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1355 | 
1356 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1357 |         if (info == null) {
1358 |           info = varInfo;
1359 |         }
1360 |         if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1361 |           // var.getType() can never be null, this would indicate a bug in the
1362 |           // scope creation logic.
1363 |           checkEnumInitializer(
1364 |               t, value,
1365 |               info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
1366 |         } else if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1367 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1368 |         } else {
1369 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1370 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1371 |         }
1372 |       }
1373 |     }
1374 |   }

===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(ObjectType,JSDocInfo,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 190-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Handle cases #1 and #3 in the class doc.
   */
Method code:
190 |   private void attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
191 |       ObjectType objType, JSDocInfo docInfo, @Nullable String qName) {
192 |     if (objType.isConstructor() ||
193 |         objType.isEnumType() ||
194 |         objType.isInterface()) {
195 |       // Named types.
196 |       if (objType.hasReferenceName() &&
197 |           objType.getReferenceName().equals(qName)) {

201 |           ((FunctionType) objType).getInstanceType().setJSDocInfo(
202 |               docInfo);
203 |         } else if (objType instanceof EnumType) {
204 |           ((EnumType) objType).getElementsType().setJSDocInfo(docInfo);

   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0

===== M0329 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getGreatestSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 629-649
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common
   * getGreatestSubtype implementations.
   */
Method code:
629 |   static JSType getGreatestSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
630 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
631 |       return thisType;
632 |     } else if (thisType.isUnknownType() || thatType.isUnknownType()) {
633 |       // The greatest subtype with any unknown type is the universal
634 |       // unknown type, unless the two types are equal.
635 |       return thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType) ? thisType :
636 |           thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
637 |     } else if (thisType.isSubtype(thatType)) {
638 |       return filterNoResolvedType(thisType);
639 |     } else if (thatType.isSubtype(thisType)) {
640 |       return filterNoResolvedType(thatType);
641 |     } else if (thisType.isUnionType()) {
642 |       return ((UnionType) thisType).meet(thatType);
643 |     } else if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
644 |       return ((UnionType) thatType).meet(thisType);
645 |     } else if (thisType.isObject() && thatType.isObject()) {
646 |       return thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_OBJECT_TYPE);
647 |     }
648 |     return thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE);
649 |   }

===== M0330 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 409-435
Documentation:

Method code:
409 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
410 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
411 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
412 |     switch (n.getType()) {
413 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
414 |         // normal type checking
415 |         final TypeCheck outerThis = this;
416 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
417 |         final FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
418 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
419 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
420 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
421 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
422 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
423 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
424 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
425 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
426 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
427 |         }
428 | 
429 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
430 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
431 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
432 |         break;
433 |     }
434 |     return true;
435 |   }

===== M0331 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0332 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Scope.Scope(Scope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Scope.java
Original range: 288-301
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a Scope given the parent Scope and the root node of the scope.
   * @param parent  The parent Scope. Cannot be null.
   * @param rootNode  Typically the FUNCTION node.
   */
Method code:
288 |   Scope(Scope parent, Node rootNode) {
289 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(parent);
290 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(rootNode != parent.rootNode);
291 | 
292 |     this.parent = parent;
293 |     this.rootNode = rootNode;
294 |     JSType nodeType = rootNode.getJSType();
295 |     if (nodeType != null && nodeType instanceof FunctionType) {
296 |       thisType = ((FunctionType) nodeType).getTypeOfThis();

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1375-1532
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope, boolean) */
Method code:
1375 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1376 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope, boolean forgiving) {
1377 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1378 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1379 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1380 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1381 | 
1382 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1383 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1384 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, forgiving)
1385 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1386 | 
1387 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1388 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1389 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1390 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1391 |         }
1392 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1393 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1394 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope, forgiving));
1395 | 
1396 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1397 |         return createOptionalType(
1398 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1399 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1400 | 
1401 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1402 |         return createOptionalType(
1403 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1404 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1405 | 
1406 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1407 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1408 | 
1409 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1410 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1411 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1412 | 
1413 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1414 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1415 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1416 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1417 |           builder.addAlternate(
1418 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope, false));
1419 |         }
1420 |         return builder.build();
1421 | 
1422 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1423 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1424 | 
1425 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1426 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1427 | 
1428 |       case Token.STRING:
1429 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1430 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1431 |         if (forgiving) {
1432 |           namedType.forgiveUnknownNames();
1433 |         }
1434 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1435 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1436 |         }
1437 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1438 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1439 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1440 |           if (typeList != null &&
1441 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1442 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1443 |             JSType parameterType =
1444 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1445 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1446 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1447 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1448 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1449 |               JSType indexType =
1450 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1451 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1452 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1453 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1454 |             }
1455 |           }
1456 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1457 |         } else {
1458 |           return namedType;
1459 |         }
1460 | 
1461 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1462 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1463 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1464 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1465 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1466 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1467 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1468 |           thisType =
1469 |               ObjectType.cast(
1470 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1471 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope, false)
1472 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1473 |           if (thisType == null) {
1474 |             reporter.warning(
1475 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1476 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1477 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1478 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1479 |                 sourceName,
1480 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), "", contextNode.getCharno());
1481 |           }
1482 | 
1483 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1484 |           current = current.getNext();
1485 |         }
1486 | 
1487 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1488 | 
1489 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1490 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1491 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1492 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1493 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1494 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1495 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1496 |               } else {
1497 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1498 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1499 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1500 |               }
1501 |             } else {
1502 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1503 |                   arg, sourceName, scope, false);
1504 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1505 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1506 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1507 |                   reporter.warning(
1508 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1509 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1510 |                 }
1511 |               } else {
1512 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1513 |               }
1514 |             }
1515 |           }
1516 |           current = current.getNext();
1517 |         }
1518 | 
1519 |         JSType returnType =
1520 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope, false);
1521 | 
1522 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1523 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1524 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1525 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1526 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1527 |             .build();
1528 |     }
1529 | 
1530 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1531 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1532 |   }

===== M0342 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1410-1447

/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1410 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1411 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1412 | 
1413 |     FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
1414 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1415 |     if (functionType.isInterface() || functionType.isConstructor()) {
1416 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.
1417 |           getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype().getConstructor();
1418 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1419 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1420 |           (baseConstructor.isConstructor() && functionType.isInterface() ||
1421 |            baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1422 |         compiler.report(
1423 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1424 |       }
1425 | 
1426 |       for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1427 |         boolean badImplementedType = false;
1428 |         ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1429 |         if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1430 |           FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1431 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1432 |           if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1433 |               !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1434 |             badImplementedType = true;
1435 |           }
1436 |         } else {
1437 |           badImplementedType = true;
1438 |         }
1439 |         if (badImplementedType) {
1440 |           report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1441 |         }
1442 |       }
1443 |       if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1444 |         validator.expectAllInterfacePropertiesImplemented(functionType);
1445 |       }
1446 |     }
1447 |   }

===== M0353 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 179-222
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
179 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
180 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
181 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
182 |     // show up in the type registry.
183 |     Scope newScope = null;
184 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
185 |     if (parent == null) {
186 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
187 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
188 | 
189 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
190 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
191 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
192 |     } else {
193 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
194 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
195 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
196 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
197 |     }
198 | 
199 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
200 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
201 | 
202 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
203 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
204 |     // build properties on.
205 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
206 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
207 |       if (type != null && type instanceof FunctionType) {
208 |         FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
209 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
210 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
211 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
212 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
213 |         }
214 |       }
215 |     }
216 | 
217 |     if (parent == null) {
218 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
219 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes);
220 |     }
221 |     return newScope;
222 |   }

Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

426 |     @Override
427 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
428 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
429 | 
430 |       switch (n.getType()) {
431 |         case Token.CALL:
432 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
433 |           break;
434 | 
435 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
436 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
437 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);

444 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
445 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();

===== M0390 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }
