===== M0395 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1626-1651
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1626 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1627 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1628 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1629 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1630 |       skipEOLs();
1631 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1632 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1633 |       skipEOLs();
1634 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1635 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1636 |       skipEOLs();
1637 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1638 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1639 |       String string = stream.getString();
1640 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1641 |         skipEOLs();
1642 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1643 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1644 |         return newStringNode(string);
1645 |       } else {
1646 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1647 |       }
1648 |     }
1649 | 
1650 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1651 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineName(Node,Node,Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 693-730
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a variable based on the {@link Token#NAME} node passed.
     * @param name The {@link Token#NAME} node.
     * @param var The parent of the {@code name} node, which must be a
     *     {@link Token#VAR} node.
     * @param parent {@code var}'s parent.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} information relating to this
     *     {@code name} node.
     */
Method code:
693 |     private void defineName(Node name, Node var, Node parent, JSDocInfo info) {
694 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
695 | 
696 |       // variable's type
697 |       JSType type = null;
698 | 
699 |       if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
700 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
701 |               (FunctionType) value.getJSType(), info, name)) {
702 |         type = value.getJSType();
703 |       }
704 | 
705 |       if (type == null) {
706 |         if (info == null) {
707 |           // the variable's type will be inferred
708 |           CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
709 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(input, sourceName);
710 |           type = input.isExtern() ?
711 |               getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : null;
712 |         } else if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
713 |           if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
714 |             // If this is an object literal, than an enum type
715 |             // has already been created for it.
716 |             type = value.getJSType();
717 |           } else {
718 |             type = createEnumTypeFromNodes(
719 |                 value, name.getString(), info, name);
720 |           }
721 |         } else if (info.isConstructor()) {
722 |           type = createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
723 |               value, name.getString(), info, name);
724 |         } else {
725 |           type = getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, name, info);
726 |         }
727 |       }
728 | 
729 |       defineSlot(name, var, type);
730 |     }

===== M0417 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 632-671
Documentation:

Method code:
632 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
633 |     String varName = n.getString();
634 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
635 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
636 |     if (value != null) {
637 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
638 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
639 |           getJSType(value));
640 |       return scope;
641 |     } else {
642 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
643 |       if (var != null) {
644 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
645 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
646 | 
647 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
648 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
649 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
650 |         boolean unflowable =
651 |             isInferred && unflowableVarNames.contains(varName);
652 | 
653 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
654 |         // inferred variable.
655 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
656 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
657 |             isInferred &&
658 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
659 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
660 | 
661 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
662 |           type = var.getType();
663 |           if (type == null) {
664 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
665 |           }
666 |         }
667 |       }
668 |     }
669 |     n.setJSType(type);
670 |     return scope;
671 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 471-515
Documentation:

Method code:
471 |     private void attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
472 |       switch (n.getType()) {
473 |         case Token.NULL:
474 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NULL_TYPE));
475 |           break;
476 | 
477 |         case Token.VOID:
478 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
479 |           break;
480 | 
481 |         case Token.STRING:
482 |           // Defer keys to the Token.OBJECTLIT case
483 |           if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
484 |             n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
485 |           }
486 |           break;
487 | 
488 |         case Token.NUMBER:
489 |           // Defer keys to the Token.OBJECTLIT case
490 |           if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
491 |             n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
492 |           }
493 |           break;
494 | 
495 |         case Token.TRUE:
496 |         case Token.FALSE:
497 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
498 |           break;
499 | 
500 |         case Token.REGEXP:
501 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));
502 |           break;
503 | 
504 |         case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
505 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
506 |           break;
507 | 
508 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
509 |           defineObjectLiteral(t, n);
510 |           break;
511 | 
512 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): If we ever support Array tuples,
513 |           // we will need to put ARRAYLIT here as well.
514 |       }
515 |     }

===== M0428 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1192-1221
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1192 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1193 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1194 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1195 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1196 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1197 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1198 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1199 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1200 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1201 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1202 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1203 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1204 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1205 |       return false;
1206 |     }
1207 | 
1208 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1209 |     if (type == null) {
1210 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1211 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1212 |       if (var != null) {
1213 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1214 |         if (varType != null) {
1215 |           type = varType;
1216 |         }
1217 |       }
1218 |     }
1219 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1220 |     return true;
1221 |   }

185 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
186 |     // Verify the nodes.

/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);

170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);

175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);

===== M0441 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0456 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 548-590
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
548 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
549 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
550 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
551 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
552 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
553 |     if (objectType == null) {
554 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
555 |     } else {
556 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
557 |         return;
558 |       }
559 | 
560 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
561 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
562 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
563 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
564 |         //
565 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
566 |         //    inferred type.
567 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
568 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
569 |         //    define it.
570 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
571 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
572 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
573 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
574 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
575 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
576 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, rightType, false, getprop);
577 |           } else {
578 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, false, getprop);
579 |           }
580 |         } else {
581 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.THIS &&
582 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
583 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, false, getprop);
584 |           } else {
585 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
586 |           }
587 |         }
588 |       }
589 |     }
590 |   }

===== M0461 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 497-543
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
497 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
498 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
499 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
500 |     switch (left.getType()) {
501 |       case Token.NAME:
502 |         String varName = left.getString();
503 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
504 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
505 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
506 |         }
507 | 
508 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
509 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
510 |         // This has two purposes:
511 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
512 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
513 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
514 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
515 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
516 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
517 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
518 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
519 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
520 |           redeclare(scope, varName, resultType);
521 |         }
522 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
523 |             resultType : null);
524 | 
525 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
526 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
527 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
528 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
529 |         }
530 |         break;
531 |       case Token.GETPROP:
532 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
533 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
534 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
535 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
536 |               resultType);
537 |         }
538 | 
539 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
540 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
541 |         break;
542 |     }
543 |   }

===== M0465 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1770-1794
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1770 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1771 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1772 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1773 |             type instanceof FunctionType ||
1774 |             type.isUnknownType());
1775 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1776 |     if (info != null) {
1777 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1778 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1779 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1780 |         type = infoType;
1781 |       }
1782 | 
1783 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1784 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1785 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1786 |         compiler.report(
1787 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1788 |       }
1789 |     }
1790 | 
1791 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1792 |       n.setJSType(type);
1793 |     }
1794 |   }

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.
   *
   * @see #analyze()
   */
Method code:
120 |   DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N> targetCfg, JoinOp<L> joinOp) {
121 |     this.cfg = targetCfg;
122 |     this.joinOp = joinOp;
123 |     Comparator<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nodeComparator =
124 |       cfg.getOptionalNodeComparator(isForward());
125 |     if (nodeComparator != null) {
126 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newTreeSet(nodeComparator);
127 |     } else {
128 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
129 |     }
130 |   }

===== M0467 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 497-552
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   */
Method code:
497 |   void expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, Node n, Node parent, Var var,
498 |       String variableName, JSType newType) {
499 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
500 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
501 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
502 |       if (info == null) {
503 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
504 |       }
505 |       allowDupe =
506 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
510 | 
511 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
512 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
513 |     // compilation process.
514 |     if (varType != null &&
515 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
516 |         newType != null &&
517 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
518 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
519 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
520 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
521 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
522 |       // native type.
523 |       if (var.input == null) {
524 |         n.setJSType(varType);
525 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
526 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
527 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
528 |           }
529 |         } else {
530 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
531 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
532 |         }
533 |       } else {
534 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
535 |         // match the original type.
536 |         //
537 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
538 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
539 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
540 |               var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) ||
541 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
542 |           if (shouldReport) {
543 |             compiler.report(
544 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
545 |                     variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
546 |                     String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
547 |                     varType.toString()));
548 |           }
549 |         }
550 |       }
551 |     }
552 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1391-1421
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1391 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1392 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1393 |         Node n = stub.node;
1394 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1395 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1396 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1397 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1398 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1399 | 
1400 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1401 |           continue;
1402 |         }
1403 | 
1404 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1405 |         // in the type registry.
1406 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1407 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1408 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1409 | 
1410 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1411 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1412 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1413 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1414 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1415 |               propName, unknownType, isExtern, n);
1416 |         } else {
1417 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1418 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1419 |         }
1420 |       }
1421 |     }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
85 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
86 |                            String sourceString,
87 |                            Config config,
88 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
89 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
90 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
91 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
92 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
93 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
94 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
95 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
96 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
97 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
98 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
99 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
100 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
104 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
105 |     try {
106 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
107 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
108 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
109 |     } finally {
110 |       Context.exit();
111 |     }
112 |     Node root = null;
113 |     if (astRoot != null) {
114 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
115 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
116 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
117 |     }
118 |     return root;
119 |   }

/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
586 |   public JSType getLeastSupertype(JSType that) {
587 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
588 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
589 |       return that.getLeastSupertype(this);
590 |     }
591 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);
592 |   }

164 |     Property p = properties.get(property);
165 |     if (p == null) {
166 |       ObjectType implicitPrototype = getImplicitPrototype();
167 |       if (implicitPrototype != null) {
168 |         return implicitPrototype.isPropertyTypeDeclared(property);
169 |       }
170 |       // property does not exist
171 |       return false;
172 |     }
173 |     return !p.inferred;
174 |   }

85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 |

97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 |

104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);

===== M0503 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 893-920
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
893 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
894 |     // unknown
895 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
896 |       return true;
897 |     }
898 |     // equality
899 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
900 |       return true;
901 |     }
902 |     // all type
903 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
904 |       return true;
905 |     }
906 |     // unions
907 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
908 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
909 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
910 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
911 |           return true;
912 |         }
913 |       }
914 |     }
915 |     // named types
916 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
917 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
918 |     }
919 |     return false;
920 |   }

Original range: 1154-1213
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1154 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
1155 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1156 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1157 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1158 |       if (relationship != null) {
1159 |         ObjectType superClass = ObjectType.cast(
1160 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.superclassName));
1161 |         ObjectType subClass = ObjectType.cast(
1162 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.subclassName));
1163 |         if (superClass != null && subClass != null) {
1164 |           FunctionType superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1165 |           FunctionType subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1166 | 
1167 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS) {
1168 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1169 |           }
1170 | 
1171 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1172 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1173 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1174 |           }
1175 |         }
1176 |       }
1177 | 
1178 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1179 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1180 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1181 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1182 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1183 |         if (objectType != null) {
1184 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1185 | 
1186 |           if (functionType != null) {
1187 |             FunctionType getterType =
1188 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1189 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1190 |                 objectType);
1191 |           }
1192 |         }
1193 |       }
1194 | 
1195 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1196 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1197 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1198 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1199 |       }
1200 | 
1201 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1202 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(t, n);
1203 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1204 |         ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1205 |             typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1206 |         if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1207 |           setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1208 |         } else {
1209 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1210 |                   CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
1211 |         }
1212 |       }
1213 |     }

===== M0507 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 205-229
Documentation:

Method code:
205 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
206 |     JSDocInfo jsDocInfo = handleJsDoc(node);
207 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
208 |     if (jsDocInfo != null) {
209 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(jsDocInfo);
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
213 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
214 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
215 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
216 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
217 |     } else {
218 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
219 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
220 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
221 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
222 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
223 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
224 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
225 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
226 |       }
227 |     }
228 |     return irNode;
229 |   }

36 |     public final L apply(List<L> values) {

879 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this);

883 | 
884 |     // Warning about typeOfThis if it doesn't resolve to an ObjectType
885 |     // is handled further upstream.
886 |     //
887 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Handle this correctly if we have a UnionType.
888 |     //
889 |     // TODO(nicksantos): In ES3, the runtime coerces "null" to the global
890 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
891 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the
892 |     // fact that when most people write @this {Foo}, they really don't
893 |     // mean "nullable Foo". For certain tags (like @extends) we de-nullify
894 |     // the name for them.

901 |     }

905 |         ImmutableList.builder();
906 |     for (ObjectType iface : implementedInterfaces) {
907 |       ObjectType resolvedIface = (ObjectType) iface.resolve(t, scope);
908 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
909 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
910 |     }
911 |     if (changed) {
912 |       implementedInterfaces = resolvedInterfaces.build();
913 |     }
914 | 
915 |     if (subTypes != null) {
916 |       for (int i = 0; i < subTypes.size(); i++) {
917 |         subTypes.set(i, (FunctionType) subTypes.get(i).resolve(t, scope));
918 |       }

920 |

922 |   }

===== M0528 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1467-1502
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1467 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1468 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1469 | 
1470 |     skipEOLs();
1471 | 
1472 |     boolean restArg = false;
1473 |     token = next();
1474 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1475 |       token = next();
1476 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1477 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1478 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1479 |       }
1480 |       restArg = true;
1481 |     }
1482 | 
1483 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1484 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1485 |       skipEOLs();
1486 |       if (restArg) {
1487 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1488 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1489 |         next();
1490 |         skipEOLs();
1491 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1492 |       }
1493 | 
1494 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1495 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1496 |       } else {
1497 |         next();
1498 |       }
1499 |     }
1500 | 
1501 |     return typeNode;
1502 |   }

===== M0554 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 830-935
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.getType() == Token.ASSIGN</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
831 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
832 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
833 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
834 | 
835 |     if (lvalue.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
836 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
837 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
838 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
839 | 
840 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
841 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
842 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
843 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
844 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
845 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
846 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
847 |         }
848 |       }
849 | 
850 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
851 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
852 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
853 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
854 |             property, rvalue);
855 |         return;
856 |       }
857 | 
858 |       // /** @enum ... */object.name = ...;
859 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
860 |         checkEnumInitializer(
861 |             t, rvalue, info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(
862 |                 t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
863 |         return;
864 |       }
865 | 
866 |       // object.prototype = ...;
867 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
868 |         if (objectJsType instanceof FunctionType) {
869 |           FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) objectJsType;
870 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
871 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
872 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
873 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
874 |           }
875 |         } else {
876 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
877 |         }
878 |         return;
879 |       }
880 | 
881 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
882 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
883 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
884 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
885 | 
886 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
887 |           JSType jsType = object2.getJSType();
888 |           if (jsType instanceof FunctionType) {
889 |             FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) jsType;
890 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
891 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
892 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
893 |             }
894 |           } else {
895 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
896 |           }
897 |           return;
898 |         }
899 |       }
900 | 
901 |       // object.property = ...;
902 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
903 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
904 |       if (type != null) {
905 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
906 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
907 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
908 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
909 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
910 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
911 |         }
912 |         return;
913 |       }
914 |     } else if (lvalue.getType() == Token.NAME) {
915 |       // variable with inferred type case
916 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
917 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
918 |       if (var != null) {
919 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
920 |           return;
921 |         }
922 |       }
923 |     }
924 | 
925 |     // fall through case
926 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
927 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
928 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
929 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
930 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
931 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
932 |     } else {
933 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
934 |     }
935 |   }

===== M0555 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 555-569
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
557 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
558 |       return scope;
559 |     }
560 | 
561 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
562 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
563 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
564 |       scopes.push(scope);
565 |     }
566 |     scopeRoots.clear();
567 | 
568 |     return scope;
569 |   }

===== M0557 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.toString()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 675-717
Documentation:
/**
   * Informally, a function is represented by
   * {@code function (params): returnType} where the {@code params} is a comma
   * separated list of types, the first one being a special
   * {@code this:T} if the function expects a known type for {@code this}.
   */
Method code:
675 |   @Override
676 |   public String toString() {
677 |     if (this == registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE)) {
678 |       return "Function";
679 |     }
680 | 
681 |     StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(32);
682 |     b.append("function (");
683 |     int paramNum = call.parameters.getChildCount();
684 |     boolean hasKnownTypeOfThis = !typeOfThis.isUnknownType();
685 |     if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
686 |       if (isConstructor()) {
687 |         b.append("new:");
688 |       } else {
689 |         b.append("this:");
690 |       }
691 |       b.append(typeOfThis.toString());
692 |     }
693 |     if (paramNum > 0) {
694 |       if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
695 |         b.append(", ");
696 |       }
697 |       Node p = call.parameters.getFirstChild();
698 |       if (p.isVarArgs()) {
699 |         appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
700 |       } else {
701 |         b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
702 |       }
703 |       p = p.getNext();
704 |       while (p != null) {
705 |         b.append(", ");
706 |         if (p.isVarArgs()) {
707 |           appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
708 |         } else {
709 |           b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
710 |         }
711 |         p = p.getNext();
712 |       }
713 |     }
714 |     b.append("): ");
715 |     b.append(call.returnType);
716 |     return b.toString();
717 |   }

===== M0564 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 675-772
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
675 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
676 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
677 |     /*
678 |      * This is the case where:
679 |      *
680 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
681 |      * the script.
682 |      *
683 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
684 |      * the caller of the function.
685 |      *
686 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
687 |      * back to the caller of the function.
688 |      *
689 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
690 |      * asked to traverse.
691 |      *
692 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
693 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
694 |      */
695 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
696 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
697 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
698 |       return null;
699 |     }
700 | 
701 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
702 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
703 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
704 |       case Token.IF:
705 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
706 |       case Token.CASE:
707 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
708 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
709 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
710 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
711 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
712 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
713 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
714 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
715 |           } else {
716 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
717 |           }
718 |         } else {
719 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
720 |         }
721 |         break;
722 |       case Token.FOR:
723 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
724 |           return parent;
725 |         } else {
726 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
727 |         }
728 |       case Token.WHILE:
729 |       case Token.DO:
730 |         return parent;
731 |       case Token.TRY:
732 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
733 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
734 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
735 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
736 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
737 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
738 |           }
739 |         // CATCH block.
740 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
743 |           } else {
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
747 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
748 |           if (cfa != null) {
749 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
750 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
751 |             }
752 |           }
753 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
754 |         }
755 |     }
756 | 
757 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
758 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
759 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
760 | 
761 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
762 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
763 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
764 |     }
765 | 
766 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
767 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
768 |     } else {
769 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
770 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |     }
772 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.isPropertyTest(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1292-1318
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is testing for the existence of a property.
   * If true, we will not emit warnings about a missing property.
   *
   * @param getProp The GETPROP being tested.
   */
Method code:
1292 |   private boolean isPropertyTest(Node getProp) {
1293 |     Node parent = getProp.getParent();
1294 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1295 |       case Token.CALL:
1296 |         return parent.getFirstChild() != getProp &&
1297 |             compiler.getCodingConvention().isPropertyTestFunction(parent);
1298 | 
1299 |       case Token.IF:
1300 |       case Token.WHILE:
1301 |       case Token.DO:
1302 |       case Token.FOR:
1303 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) == getProp;
1304 | 
1305 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1306 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1307 |         return true;
1308 | 
1309 |       case Token.AND:
1310 |       case Token.HOOK:
1311 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == getProp;
1312 | 
1313 |       case Token.NOT:
1314 |         return parent.getParent().getType() == Token.OR &&
1315 |             parent.getParent().getFirstChild() == parent;
1316 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 222-258
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
222 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
223 |     this.options = options;
224 |     if (errorManager == null) {
225 |       if (outStream == null) {
226 |         setErrorManager(
227 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
228 |       } else {
229 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
230 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
231 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
232 |         setErrorManager(printer);
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
237 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
238 |     guards.add(
239 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
240 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
241 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
242 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
243 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
244 |     }

256 |     }
257 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
258 |   }

===== M0592 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)

172 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType object) {

179 |         returnTypeInferred != that.returnTypeInferred) {
180 |       return false;
181 |     }

183 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(FunctionType,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 739-749
Documentation:
/**
     * If a variable is assigned a function literal in the global scope,
     * make that a declared type (even if there's no doc info).
     * There's only one exception to this rule:
     * if the return type is inferred, and we're in a local
     * scope, we should assume the whole function is inferred.
     */
Method code:
739 |     private boolean shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
740 |         FunctionType type, JSDocInfo info, Node lValue) {
741 |       if (info != null) {
742 |         return true;
743 |       }
744 |       if (lValue != null &&
745 |           NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(lValue, lValue.getParent())) {

===== M0600 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param owner The owner of the property being assigned to.
   * @param propName The name of the property being assigned to.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
331 |   boolean expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
332 |       JSType leftType, Node owner, String propName) {
333 |     // The NoType check is a hack to make typedefs work ok.
334 |     if (!leftType.isNoType() && !rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
335 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
336 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
337 |         // the line numbers of both types.

342 |             getReadableJSTypeName(owner, true),

345 |       return false;
346 |     }
347 |     return true;
348 |   }

===== M0620 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 347-391
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
347 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
348 |     if (info != null) {
349 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
350 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
351 | 
352 |       // base type
353 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
354 |         if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
355 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
356 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
357 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
358 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
359 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
360 |           }
361 |         } else {
362 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
363 |         }
364 |       }
365 | 
366 |       // implemented interfaces
367 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
368 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
369 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
370 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
371 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
372 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
373 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
374 |           }
375 |         }
376 |         if (baseType != null) {
377 |           JSType maybeFunctionType = baseType.getConstructor();
378 |           if (maybeFunctionType instanceof FunctionType) {
379 |             FunctionType functionType = baseType.getConstructor();
380 |             Iterables.addAll(
381 |                 implementedInterfaces,
382 |                 functionType.getImplementedInterfaces());
383 |           }
384 |         }
385 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
386 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
387 |       }
388 |     }
389 | 
390 |     return this;
391 |   }

Method code:
1697 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
1698 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1699 |     if (info == null) {
1700 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
1701 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
1702 |       if (parentType == Token.NAME) {
1703 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
1704 |         if (info == null && parent.getParent().hasOneChild()) {
1705 |           info = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
1706 |         }
1707 |       } else if (parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
1708 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
1709 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
1710 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
1711 |       }
1712 |     }
1713 |     return info;
1714 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
447 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
448 |     JSType childType;
449 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
450 |     Node left, right;
451 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
452 |     boolean typeable = true;
453 | 
454 |     switch (n.getType()) {
455 |       case Token.NAME:
456 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
457 |         break;
458 | 
459 |       case Token.LP:
460 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
461 |         // ignored here.
462 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
463 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
464 |         } else {
465 |           typeable = false;
466 |         }
467 |         break;
468 | 
469 |       case Token.COMMA:
470 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.TRUE:
474 |       case Token.FALSE:
475 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
476 |         break;
477 | 
478 |       case Token.THIS:
479 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
480 |         break;
481 | 
482 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
483 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
484 |         break;
485 | 
486 |       case Token.GET_REF:
487 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
488 |         break;
489 | 
490 |       case Token.NULL:
491 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
492 |         break;
493 | 
494 |       case Token.NUMBER:
495 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
496 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
497 |           ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
498 |         }
499 |         break;
500 | 
501 |       case Token.STRING:
502 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
503 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
504 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
505 |         }
506 |         break;
507 | 
508 |       case Token.GET:
509 |       case Token.SET:
510 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
511 |         break;
512 | 
513 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
514 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
515 |         break;
516 | 
517 |       case Token.REGEXP:
518 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
519 |         break;
520 | 
521 |       case Token.GETPROP:
522 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
523 |         typeable = !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
524 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
525 |         break;
526 | 
527 |       case Token.GETELEM:
528 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
529 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
530 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
531 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
532 |         typeable = false;
533 |         break;
534 | 
535 |       case Token.VAR:
536 |         visitVar(t, n);
537 |         typeable = false;
538 |         break;
539 | 
540 |       case Token.NEW:
541 |         visitNew(t, n);
542 |         typeable = true;
543 |         break;
544 | 
545 |       case Token.CALL:
546 |         visitCall(t, n);
547 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
548 |         break;
549 | 
550 |       case Token.RETURN:
551 |         visitReturn(t, n);
552 |         typeable = false;
553 |         break;
554 | 
555 |       case Token.DEC:
556 |       case Token.INC:
557 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
558 |         validator.expectNumber(
559 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
560 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
561 |         break;
562 | 
563 |       case Token.NOT:
564 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
565 |         break;
566 | 
567 |       case Token.VOID:
568 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
572 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
573 |         break;
574 | 
575 |       case Token.BITNOT:
576 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
577 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
578 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
579 |               childType.toString());
580 |         }
581 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.POS:
585 |       case Token.NEG:
586 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
587 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
588 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
589 |         break;
590 | 
591 |       case Token.EQ:
592 |       case Token.NE: {
593 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
594 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
595 | 
596 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
597 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
598 |         TernaryValue result =
599 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
600 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
601 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
602 |             result = result.not();
603 |           }
604 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
605 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
606 |         }
607 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
608 |         break;
609 |       }
610 | 
611 |       case Token.SHEQ:
612 |       case Token.SHNE: {
613 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
614 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
615 | 
616 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
617 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
618 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
619 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
620 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
621 |               rightType.toString());
622 |         }
623 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
624 |         break;
625 |       }
626 | 
627 |       case Token.LT:
628 |       case Token.LE:
629 |       case Token.GT:
630 |       case Token.GE:
631 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
632 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
633 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
634 |           validator.expectNumber(
635 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
636 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
637 |           validator.expectNumber(
638 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
639 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
640 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
641 |           // OK.
642 |         } else {
643 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
644 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
645 |           // should match a string context.
646 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
647 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
648 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
649 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
650 |           message = "right side of comparison";
651 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
652 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
653 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
654 |         }
655 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
656 |         break;
657 | 
658 |       case Token.IN:
659 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
660 |         right = n.getLastChild();
661 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
662 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
663 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
664 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
665 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
666 |         break;
667 | 
668 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
669 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
670 |         right = n.getLastChild();
671 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
672 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
673 | 
674 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
675 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
676 |         validator.expectActualObject(
677 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
678 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
679 |         break;
680 | 
681 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
682 |         visitAssign(t, n);
683 |         typeable = false;
684 |         break;
685 | 
686 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
687 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
688 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
689 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
690 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
691 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
692 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
693 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
694 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
695 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
696 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
697 |       case Token.LSH:
698 |       case Token.RSH:
699 |       case Token.URSH:
700 |       case Token.DIV:
701 |       case Token.MOD:
702 |       case Token.BITOR:
703 |       case Token.BITXOR:
704 |       case Token.BITAND:
705 |       case Token.SUB:
706 |       case Token.ADD:
707 |       case Token.MUL:
708 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
709 |         break;
710 | 
711 |       case Token.DELPROP:
712 |         if (!isReference(n.getFirstChild())) {
713 |           report(t, n, BAD_DELETE);
714 |         }
715 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
716 |         break;
717 | 
718 |       case Token.CASE:
719 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
720 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
721 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
722 |         typeable = false;
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       case Token.WITH: {
726 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
727 |         childType = getJSType(child);
728 |         validator.expectObject(
729 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
730 |         typeable = false;
731 |         break;
732 |       }
733 | 
734 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
735 |         visitFunction(t, n);
736 |         break;
737 | 
738 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
739 |       case Token.LABEL:
740 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
741 |       case Token.SWITCH:
742 |       case Token.BREAK:
743 |       case Token.CATCH:
744 |       case Token.TRY:
745 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
746 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
747 |       case Token.BLOCK:
748 |       case Token.EMPTY:
749 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
750 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
751 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
752 |       case Token.THROW:
753 |         typeable = false;
754 |         break;
755 | 
756 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
757 |       case Token.DO:
758 |       case Token.FOR:
759 |       case Token.IF:
760 |       case Token.WHILE:
761 |         typeable = false;
762 |         break;
763 | 
764 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
765 |       case Token.AND:
766 |       case Token.HOOK:
767 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
768 |       case Token.OR:
769 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
770 |           ensureTyped(t, n);

773 |           if ((n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT)

784 |         }

789 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
790 |         break;
791 |     }
792 | 
793 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
794 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
795 | 
796 |     if (typeable) {
797 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
798 |     }
799 | 
800 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
801 |   }

Method code:
382 |   private void checkNoTypeCheckSection(Node n, boolean enterSection) {
383 |     switch (n.getType()) {
384 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
385 |       case Token.BLOCK:
386 |       case Token.VAR:
387 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
388 |       case Token.ASSIGN:

1649 |     private void handleFunctionInputs(Node fnNode) {
1650 |       // Handle bleeding functions.
1651 |       Node fnNameNode = fnNode.getFirstChild();
1652 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
1653 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty()) {
1654 |         Scope.Var fnVar = scope.getVar(fnName);
1655 |         if (fnVar == null ||
1656 |             // Make sure we're not touching a native function. Native
1657 |             // functions aren't bleeding, but may not have a declaration
1658 |             // node.

1660 |                 // Make sure that the function is actually bleeding by checking
1661 |                 // if has already been declared.
1662 |                 fnVar.getInitialValue() != fnNode)) {

1664 |         }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType>)

Original range: 865-889
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve all the unresolved types in the given scope.
   */
Method code:
865 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
866 |     for (NamedType type : unresolvedNamedTypes.get(scope)) {
867 |       type.resolve(reporter, scope);
868 |     }
869 | 
870 |     resolvedNamedTypes.putAll(scope, unresolvedNamedTypes.removeAll(scope));
871 | 
872 |     if (scope != null && scope.getParentScope() == null) {
873 |       // By default, the global "this" type is just an anonymous object.
874 |       // If the user has defined a Window type, make the Window the
875 |       // implicit prototype of "this".
876 |       PrototypeObjectType globalThis = (PrototypeObjectType) getNativeType(
877 |           JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
878 |       JSType windowType = getType("Window");
879 |       if (globalThis.isUnknownType()) {
880 |         ObjectType windowObjType = ObjectType.cast(windowType);
881 |         if (windowObjType != null) {
882 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(windowObjType);
883 |         } else {
884 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(
885 |               getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE));
886 |         }
887 |       }
888 |     }
889 |   }
