===== M0680 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {

===== M0689 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverse(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 306-466
Documentation:

Method code:
306 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
307 |     switch (n.getType()) {
308 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
309 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
310 |         break;
311 | 
312 |       case Token.NAME:
313 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
314 |         break;
315 | 
316 |       case Token.GETPROP:
317 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
318 |         break;
319 | 
320 |       case Token.AND:
321 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
322 |             .createChildFlowScope();
323 |         break;
324 | 
325 |       case Token.OR:
326 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
327 |             .createChildFlowScope();
328 |         break;
329 | 
330 |       case Token.HOOK:
331 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
332 |         break;
333 | 
334 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
335 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
336 |         break;
337 | 
338 |       case Token.CALL:
339 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
340 |         break;
341 | 
342 |       case Token.NEW:
343 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
344 |         break;
345 | 
346 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
347 |       case Token.ADD:
348 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
349 |         break;
350 | 
351 |       case Token.POS:
352 |       case Token.NEG:
353 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
354 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
355 |         break;
356 | 
357 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
358 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
359 |         break;
360 | 
361 |       case Token.THIS:
362 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
363 |         break;
364 | 
365 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
366 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
367 |       case Token.LSH:
368 |       case Token.RSH:
369 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
370 |       case Token.URSH:
371 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
372 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
373 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
374 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
375 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
376 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
377 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
378 |       case Token.DIV:
379 |       case Token.MOD:
380 |       case Token.BITAND:
381 |       case Token.BITXOR:
382 |       case Token.BITOR:
383 |       case Token.MUL:
384 |       case Token.SUB:
385 |       case Token.DEC:
386 |       case Token.INC:
387 |       case Token.BITNOT:
388 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
389 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
390 |         break;
391 | 
392 |       case Token.LP:
393 |       case Token.GET_REF:
394 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
395 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
396 |         break;
397 | 
398 |       case Token.COMMA:
399 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
400 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
401 |         break;
402 | 
403 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
404 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
405 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
406 |         break;
407 | 
408 |       case Token.LT:
409 |       case Token.LE:
410 |       case Token.GT:
411 |       case Token.GE:
412 |       case Token.NOT:
413 |       case Token.EQ:
414 |       case Token.NE:
415 |       case Token.SHEQ:
416 |       case Token.SHNE:
417 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
418 |       case Token.IN:
419 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
420 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
421 |         break;
422 | 
423 |       case Token.GETELEM:
424 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
425 |         break;
426 | 
427 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
428 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
429 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
430 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
431 |         }
432 |         break;
433 | 
434 |       case Token.SWITCH:
435 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
436 |         break;
437 | 
438 |       case Token.VAR:
439 |       case Token.RETURN:
440 |       case Token.THROW:
441 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.CATCH:
445 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
446 |         break;
447 |     }
448 |     if (n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
449 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
450 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
451 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
452 | 
453 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
454 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
455 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
456 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
457 |             n.getParent().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
458 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
459 |         }
460 | 
461 |         n.setJSType(castType);
462 |       }
463 |     }
464 | 
465 |     return scope;
466 |   }

===== M0691 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0710 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 69-121
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
69 |   @Override
70 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
71 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
72 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
73 |     if (type != null) {
74 |       Node subclass = null;
75 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
76 | 
77 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
78 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
79 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
80 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
81 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
82 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
83 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
84 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
85 |           callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP;
86 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
87 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
88 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
89 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
90 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
91 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
95 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to inheritance.
96 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
97 |           return null;
98 |         }
99 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
100 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
101 |             return null;
102 |           }
103 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
104 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
105 |         }
106 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
107 |       }
108 | 
109 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
110 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
111 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
112 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
113 |       if (subclass != null &&
114 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
115 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
116 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
117 |       }
118 |     }
119 | 
120 |     return null;
121 |   }

===== M0714 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 252-302
Documentation:

Method code:
252 |   @Override
253 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
254 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
255 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
256 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
257 |         return true;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
261 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
262 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
263 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
264 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
265 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
266 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
267 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
268 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
269 |         return false;
270 |       }
271 | 
272 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
273 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
274 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
275 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
276 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
277 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
278 |             return false;
279 |           }
280 |         }
281 | 
282 |         return true;
283 |       }
284 | 
285 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
286 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
287 | 
288 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
289 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
290 |           return false;
291 |         }
292 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
293 |       }
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |       }
299 |       return true;
300 |     }
301 |     return false;
302 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.

453 |       }
454 |     }
455 |     return false;
456 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 396-412
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
396 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
397 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
398 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
399 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
400 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
401 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
402 |         String inputName = input.getName();
403 | 
404 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
405 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
406 |         // will get an error down the line.
407 |         inputs.add(input);
408 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
409 |       }
410 |     }
411 |     return inputs;
412 |   }

===== M0730 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getPropertyType(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 374-430
Documentation:

Method code:
374 |   @Override
375 |   public JSType getPropertyType(String name) {
376 |     if ("prototype".equals(name)) {
377 |       return getPrototype();
378 |     } else {
379 |       if (!hasOwnProperty(name)) {
380 |         if ("call".equals(name)) {
381 |           // Define the "call" function lazily.
382 |           Node params = getParametersNode();
383 |           if (params == null) {
384 |             // If there's no params array, don't do any type-checking
385 |             // in this CALL function.
386 |             defineDeclaredProperty(name,
387 |                 new FunctionBuilder(registry)
388 |                     .withReturnType(getReturnType())
389 |                     .build(),
390 |                 false, source);
391 |           } else {
392 |             params = params.cloneTree();
393 |             Node thisTypeNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, "thisType");
394 |             thisTypeNode.setJSType(
395 |                 registry.createOptionalNullableType(getTypeOfThis()));
396 |             params.addChildToFront(thisTypeNode);
397 |             thisTypeNode.setOptionalArg(true);
398 | 
399 |             defineDeclaredProperty(name,
400 |                 new FunctionBuilder(registry)
401 |                     .withParamsNode(params)
402 |                     .withReturnType(getReturnType())
403 |                     .build(),
404 |                 false, source);
405 |           }
406 |         } else if ("apply".equals(name)) {
407 |           // Define the "apply" function lazily.
408 |           FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(registry);
409 | 
410 |           // Ecma-262 says that apply's second argument must be an Array
411 |           // or an arguments object. We don't model the arguments object,
412 |           // so let's just be forgiving for now.
413 |           // TODO(nicksantos): Model the Arguments object.
414 |           builder.addOptionalParams(
415 |               registry.createNullableType(getTypeOfThis()),
416 |               registry.createNullableType(
417 |                   registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE)));
418 | 
419 |           defineDeclaredProperty(name,
420 |               new FunctionBuilder(registry)
421 |                   .withParams(builder)
422 |                   .withReturnType(getReturnType())
423 |                   .build(),
424 |               false, source);
425 |         }
426 |       }
427 | 
428 |       return super.getPropertyType(name);
429 |     }
430 |   }

===== M0733 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 813-840
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
813 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
814 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
815 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
816 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
817 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
818 |           return;
819 |         }
820 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
821 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
822 | 
823 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
824 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
825 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
826 |           } else {
827 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
828 |           }
829 |         } else { // Has a catch.
830 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
831 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
832 |             return;
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
835 |           }
836 |         }
837 |         lastJump = handler;
838 |       }
839 |     }
840 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 817-861
Documentation:

Method code:
817 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
818 |       Node callNode) {
819 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
820 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
821 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
822 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
823 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
824 |       return scope;
825 |     }
826 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
827 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
828 |       return scope;
829 |     }
830 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
831 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
832 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
833 |     if (assertedType == null) {
834 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
835 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
836 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
837 |         if (type != narrowed) {
838 |           scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
839 |           redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
840 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
841 |         }
842 |       } else if (assertedNode.getType() == Token.AND ||
843 |                  assertedNode.getType() == Token.OR) {
844 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
845 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
846 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
847 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
848 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
849 |       }
850 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
851 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
852 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
853 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
854 |       if (type != narrowed) {
855 |         scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
856 |         redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
857 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
858 |       }
859 |     }
860 |     return scope;
861 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.TypeInference(AbstractCompiler,ControlFlowGraph<Node>,ReverseAbstractInterpreter,Scope,Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec>,Collection<Var>)

// TODO(nicksantos): Create a builder for this class.
Method code:
119 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
120 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
121 |                 Scope functionScope,
122 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap,
123 |                 Collection<Var> unflowableVars) {
124 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
125 |     this.compiler = compiler;
126 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
127 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
128 |     this.syntacticScope = functionScope;
129 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
130 |     this.assertionFunctionsMap = assertionFunctionsMap;
131 | 
132 |     for (Var unflowableVar : unflowableVars) {
133 |       String name = unflowableVar.getName();
134 |       if (functionScope.getVar(name) == unflowableVar) {
135 |         this.unflowableVarNames.add(name);
136 |       }
137 |     }
138 | 
139 |     Iterator<Var> varIt = functionScope.getVars();
140 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
141 |       Var var = varIt.next();
142 |       if (this.unflowableVarNames.contains(var.getName())) {
143 |         continue;
144 |       }
145 | 
146 |       // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
147 |       // type is VOID.
148 |       if (var.getParentNode() != null &&
149 |           var.getType() == null && // no declared type
150 |           var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.VAR &&
151 |           !var.isExtern()) {
152 |         this.functionScope.inferSlotType(

154 |       }
155 |     }
156 | 
157 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
158 |         new Scope(functionScope.getRootNode(), functionScope.getTypeOfThis()));

===== M0773 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1581-1620
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1581 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1582 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1583 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1584 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1585 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1586 |       // {?} - right curly
1587 |       // {?=} - equals
1588 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1589 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1590 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1591 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1592 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1593 |       token = next();
1594 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1595 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1596 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1597 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1598 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1599 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1600 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1601 |       }
1602 | 
1603 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1604 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1605 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1606 |     } else {
1607 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1608 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1609 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1610 |           next();
1611 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1612 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1613 |           next();
1614 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1615 |         }
1616 |       }
1617 | 
1618 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1619 |     }
1620 |   }

===== M0781 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferThisType(JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 418-447
Documentation:
/**
   * Infers the type of {@code this}.
   * @param info The JSDocInfo for this function.
   * @param owner The node for the object whose prototype "owns" this function.
   *     For example, {@code A} in the expression {@code A.prototype.foo}. May
   *     be null to indicate that this is not a prototype property.
   */
Method code:
418 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferThisType(JSDocInfo info,
419 |       @Nullable Node owner) {
420 |     ObjectType maybeThisType = null;
421 |     if (info != null && info.hasThisType()) {
422 |       maybeThisType = ObjectType.cast(
423 |           info.getThisType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
424 |     }
425 |     if (maybeThisType != null) {
426 |       thisType = maybeThisType;
427 |       thisType.setValidator(new ThisTypeValidator());
428 |     } else if (owner != null &&
429 |                (info == null || !info.hasType())) {
430 |       // If the function is of the form:
431 |       // x.prototype.y = function() {}
432 |       // then we can assume "x" is the @this type. On the other hand,
433 |       // if it's of the form:
434 |       // /** @type {Function} */ x.prototype.y;
435 |       // then we should not give it a @this type.
436 |       String ownerTypeName = owner.getQualifiedName();
437 |       ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(
438 |           typeRegistry.getForgivingType(
439 |               scope, ownerTypeName, sourceName,
440 |               owner.getLineno(), owner.getCharno()));
441 |       if (ownerType != null) {
442 |         thisType = ownerType;
443 |       }
444 |     }
445 | 
446 |     return this;
447 |   }

===== M0794 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

===== M0812 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0836 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitGetProp(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1231-1257
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1231 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1232 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1233 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1234 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1235 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
1236 |       return;
1237 |     }
1238 | 
1239 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1240 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1241 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1242 |     // and what isn't.
1243 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1244 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1245 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1246 | 
1247 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1248 |     // non-object.
1249 |     if (!validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(t, n, childType,
1250 |             childType + " has no properties", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1251 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1252 |       return;
1253 |     }
1254 | 
1255 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1256 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1257 |   }

===== M0861 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 659-685
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infimums, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
659 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
660 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
661 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
662 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
663 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
664 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
665 |       UnionType unionType = (UnionType) type;
666 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
667 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
668 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
669 |           needsFiltering = true;
670 |           break;
671 |         }
672 |       }
673 | 
674 |       if (needsFiltering) {
675 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
676 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
677 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
678 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
679 |           }
680 |         }
681 |         return builder.build();
682 |       }
683 |     }
684 |     return type;
685 |   }
